Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 10851 after decompression) · Source record

A reformer squat variation that builds the legs. It focuses on controlled bending and straightening.

Proposed staging

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 10857 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A slow, loaded forward bend that rolls through the spine. Keep the barbell light and the motion smooth.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand holding a very light barbell in front of your thighs.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 10857.json#/instructions/0

10858 — dumbbell lu raise

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 10858 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A dumbbell raise that lifts the arms out and up in an L to U path. It builds shoulder strength and upper back control.

Proposed staging

Stand tall holding a light dumbbell in each hand, arms by your sides.

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand tall holding a light dumbbell in each hand, arms by your sides. 10858.json#/instructions/0
  2. Raise both arms out to the sides until they reach shoulder height, elbows bent to 90 degrees. 10858.json#/instructions/1
  3. Rotate your forearms upward so the dumbbells point to the ceiling. 10858.json#/instructions/2
  4. Pause, then lower back down the same path with control. 10858.json#/instructions/3
  5. Keep your ribs down and avoid shrugging. 10858.json#/instructions/4
